### `insight_up_bucket_tune_clean_hours`

How many hours in a row a bucket must go without a Roblox 429 attributed to it before the rule may propose raising it. A threshold of the UP-BUCKET-TUNE recommendation rule, in hours; the rule detects: A pacing bucket is too tight or too loose.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | UP-BUCKET-TUNE: Hours with zero 429s before proposing a raise |
| Group | Recommendation rule tuning |
| Type | int |
| Default | 24 hours |
| Range | 1 to 720 hours |
| Unit | hours |
| Step | 1 |
| If raised | Needs a longer record without Roblox 429s before proposing a higher rate, so raises are rarer and safer. |
| If lowered | Proposes raises after a shorter clean record; a higher rate is more likely to bring 429s back. |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |

| High risk when | none |

### `insight_cache_ttl_tune_identical_raise_pct`

The share of refetches (an expired cache entry fetched again) that came back with exactly the same body; at or above it the data rarely changes, and the rule proposes a longer cache time. A threshold of the CACHE-TTL-TUNE recommendation rule, in percent; the rule detects: An endpoint's cache time can safely rise, or must fall.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | CACHE-TTL-TUNE: Identical refetches needed to propose a longer cache time |
| Group | Recommendation rule tuning |
| Type | int |
| Default | 90 percent |
| Range | 50 to 100 percent |
| Unit | percent |
| Step | 1 |
| If raised | Proposes longer cache times only for data that almost never changes, so callers are less likely to get outdated answers. Keep it above the 'shorter cache time' threshold. |
| If lowered | Proposes longer cache times for data that changes more often, saving more calls but serving outdated answers more often. |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |
| Risk | low |
| High risk when | none |

### `insight_filter_collateral_served_pct`

The share of an experience's other requests that Roxy served; at or above it the experience counts as legitimate, so a rule refusing its requests may be hitting real players. A threshold of the FILTER-COLLATERAL recommendation rule, in percent; the rule detects: A filter is blocking legitimate traffic.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | FILTER-COLLATERAL: Served share that marks a place as legitimate |
| Group | Recommendation rule tuning |
| Type | int |
| Default | 95 percent |
| Range | 50 to 100 percent |
| Unit | percent |
| Step | 1 |
| If raised | Only places whose other traffic is almost all served count as legitimate, so fewer rules are reported. |
| If lowered | Places with more refused traffic also count as legitimate, so more rules are reported, including rules that are correctly blocking abuse. |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |
| Risk | low |
| High risk when | none |
| Applies | live (hot reload fleet-wide within about a second) |

### `insight_sys_loop_lag_p99_ms`

How late a worker's event loop ran its scheduled checks: only the slowest 1% of checks were later than this. Lag means something blocked the loop, so every request in that worker waited. A threshold of the SYS-LOOP-LAG recommendation rule, in ms; the rule detects: The event loop is lagging (something is blocking a worker).

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | SYS-LOOP-LAG: p99 event loop lag (99% of checks lag less) |
| Group | Recommendation rule tuning |
| Type | int |
| Default | 100 ms |
| Range | 5 to 10000 ms |
| Unit | ms |
| Step | 1 |
| If raised | Fires only on severe stalls; smaller blocking calls go unreported. |
| If lowered | Fires on small stalls, which garbage collection alone can cause. |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |
| Risk | low |
| High risk when | none |

## Public site and compatibility

### `pause_message_default`

The text callers receive, with HTTP status 503, while the proxy is paused and the admin did not type a specific reason in the pause dialog. Game developers see it in their scripts' error output, so keep it short and plain.

### `compat_collapse_upstream_errors`

Controls the status code callers get when Roblox fails or rejects a request. Off (the v2 behavior) passes the real status through, such as 404, or 429 with a Retry-After header saying how long to wait. On brings back v1 behavior, where every upstream failure became a 500.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | v1-style upstream errors (always 500) |
| Group | Public site and compatibility |
| Type | bool |
| Default | 0 |
| Range | 0 (off) or 1 (on) |
| Unit | none |
| Step | none |
| If raised | n/a |
| If lowered | n/a |
| If enabled | Every Roblox 4xx, Roblox 5xx, 502 and 504 becomes a 500 with the text 'Upstream request failed; please try again later.', and cached Roblox 404s replay as 500, exactly as in v1. Old scripts that only check for 200 or 500 keep working, but they usually retry at once, which sends more traffic to Roblox and causes more 429s. Roxy's own refusals (throttles, pause, invalid URLs) are unchanged, and Retry-After is still sent. |
| If disabled | Callers see the real upstream status (404 stays 404, 429 stays 429 with Retry-After) plus a Roxy-Upstream-Status header, so well-written scripts can wait the right amount of time instead of retrying immediately. |
| Risk | medium |
| High risk when | none |
| Applies | live (hot reload fleet-wide within about a second) |
| Dashboard | `settings#public-site` |
| Related settings | none |
| Related rules | none |
| Related recommendations | none |
| Auto-apply bounds | none |
| Sensitive | no |
| Since | 2.0 |
| v1 key | same key, or new in v2 |
| v1 default | none |
| Max length | none |
| Item max length | none |
| Item range | none |
| Pending owner verification | no |
| Notes | Owner decision D4: real upstream status by default. v1 always behaved as if this were on. The full status mapping is plan 7.13. |

### `public_cors_allow_any_origin`

CORS (cross-origin resource sharing) headers tell a web browser whether a page on another website may read Roxy's responses. Roblox game servers do not need CORS at all; only browser pages do. Off sends no CORS headers, so web pages on other sites cannot use Roxy from their visitors' browsers.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | Allow browsers on any website (CORS) |
| Group | Public site and compatibility |
| Type | bool |
| Default | 0 |
| Range | 0 (off) or 1 (on) |
| Unit | none |
| Step | none |
| If raised | n/a |
| If lowered | n/a |
| If enabled | Roxy adds 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *' to GET responses (never with credentials), so any website can call Roxy from its visitors' browsers. That turns Roxy into a free public API for the whole web, and the extra traffic counts against Roblox's rate limits for every caller. |
| If disabled | No CORS headers are sent. Roblox game servers and server-side scripts are unaffected; only browser pages on other websites are blocked from reading responses. |
| Risk | high |
| High risk when | value = 1: Browsers on any website can use Roxy as a free API, adding load that counts against Roblox's rate limits for every caller. |
| Applies | live (hot reload fleet-wide within about a second) |
| Dashboard | `settings#public-site` |
| Related settings | none |
| Related rules | none |
| Related recommendations | `SEC-DEFAULTS` |
| Auto-apply bounds | none |
| Sensitive | no |
| Since | 2.0 |
| v1 key | same key, or new in v2 |
| v1 default | none |
| Max length | none |
| Item max length | none |
| Item range | none |
| Pending owner verification | yes |
| Notes | Owner decision D20. The admin dashboard never sends CORS headers, whatever this is set to. |

### `public_status_page_enabled`

Turns the public /status page on or off. It shows only coarse health (operational, degraded, paused or maintenance), a 24-hour uptime bar and plain-language notes, with no internal details, IP addresses or counts that would help an attacker.

| If disabled | /status returns 404 Not Found. The /health JSON endpoint that uptime monitors use is not affected. |

### `site_white_hats_text`

The 'No login required' section of the home page: it explains that Roxy only handles public requests and warns visitors never to send their .ROBLOSECURITY cookie to Roxy or any other proxy.

| Default | "Roxy is built for public, unauthenticated Roblox web API calls only; it does not support requests that require you to be logged in, and it will refuse any request that carries anything that looks like a real Roblox .ROBLOSECURITY session cookie.<br><br>Never send your ROBLOSECURITY token to Roxy, or to any third-party proxy. Sharing it lets someone log in as you and take your account, Robux, and items; no exceptions, no matter how trustworthy a service looks or claims to be. Even a secure, open-source server can't prove what's actually running on it. The only safe token is one you never send." |

## Dashboard

### `ui_timezone`

The timezone for every time shown in the dashboard and for where a 'day' and a 'month' begin in long-term charts and comparisons. Use an IANA zone name such as America/New_York, Europe/London or UTC.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | Dashboard timezone |
| Group | Dashboard |
| Type | string |
| Default | "America/New_York" |
| Range | any |
| Unit | none |
| Step | none |
| If raised | n/a |
| If lowered | n/a |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |
| Risk | medium |
| High risk when | none |
| Applies | live (hot reload fleet-wide within about a second) |
| Dashboard | `user-menu#preferences` |
| Related settings | `maintenance_hour`, `alert_digest_hour`, `retention_day_days` |
| Related rules | none |
| Related recommendations | none |
| Auto-apply bounds | none |
| Sensitive | no |
| Since | 2.0 |
| v1 key | same key, or new in v2 |
| v1 default | none |
| Max length | 64 |
| Item max length | none |
| Item range | none |
| Pending owner verification | yes |
| Notes | Must be a valid IANA zone name. Changing it affects only daily and monthly totals computed after the change: older days keep the zone they were computed in, and charts that span the change show a note. Minute and hour data are always stored in UTC. The default awaits the owner's preference (plan 6.4). |

### `ui_default_theme`

The color theme the dashboard uses for admins who have not picked one in their own preferences. An admin's own choice always wins over this default.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Label | Default dashboard theme |
| Group | Dashboard |
| Type | enum |
| Default | "dark" |
| Range | one of `dark`, `light`, `system` |
| Unit | none |
| Step | none |
| If raised | n/a |
| If lowered | n/a |
| If enabled | n/a |
| If disabled | n/a |
| Risk | low |
| High risk when | none |
| Applies | live (hot reload fleet-wide within about a second) |
| Dashboard | `user-menu#preferences` |
| Related settings | `ui_timezone` |
| Related rules | none |
| Related recommendations | none |
| Auto-apply bounds | none |
| Sensitive | no |
| Since | 2.0 |
| v1 key | same key, or new in v2 |
| v1 default | none |
| Max length | none |
| Item max length | none |
| Item range | none |
| Pending owner verification | no |
| Notes | none |

Options:

- `dark` (Dark): Light text on a dark background, like the v1 dashboard and public site.
- `light` (Light): Dark text on a light background; easier to read in bright rooms and when printing.
- `system` (Follow the browser): Uses the light or dark preference of each admin's operating system or browser, and switches automatically when that preference changes.
